    41	// ****************************************************************************
    42	// Function: fill
    43	//
    44	// Purpose:
    45	//   Initialize array with random values
    46	//
    47	// Arguments:
    48	//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
    49	//   n: number of elements in the array
    50	//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
    51	//
    52	// ****************************************************************************
    53	void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    54	    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    55	        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    56	    }
    57	}
    58	
    59	// ****************************************************************************
    60	// Function: initRandomMatrix
    61	//
    62	// Purpose:
    63	//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
    64	//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
    65	//   row (CSR) format.
    66	//
    67	// Arguments:
    68	//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
    69	//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
    70	//                  last element is the index one past the last element
    71	//   n:             number of nonzero elements
    72	//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
    73	//
    74	// ****************************************************************************
    75	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    76	    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;
    77	
    78	    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    79	    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));
    80	
    81	    // Seed random number generator
    82	    srand(8675309);
    83	
    84	    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    85	    bool fillRemaining = false;
    86	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
    87	        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
    88	        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
    89	            const uint64_t numEntriesLeft =
    90	                static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) * dim -
    91	                (static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * dim + j);
    92	            const uint64_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
    93	            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
    94	                fillRemaining = true;
    95	            }
    96	            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
    97	            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
    98	                // Assign (i,j) a value
    99	                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
   100	                nnzAssigned++;
   101	            }
   102	        }
   103	    }
   104	    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
   105	    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
   106	}
   107	
   108	// ****************************************************************************
   109	// Function: spmvCpu
   110	//
   111	// Purpose:
   112	//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
   113	//
   114	// Arguments:
   115	//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
   116	//   cols: array of column indices for each element
   117	//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
   118	//                  last element is the index one past the last element
   119	//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
   120	//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
   121	//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
   122	//
   123	// ****************************************************************************
   124	void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
   125	             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
   126	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
   127	        double t = 0.0;
   128	        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
   129	            const auto col = cols[j];
   130	            t += val[j] * vec[col];
   131	        }
   132	        out[i] = t;
   133	    }
   134	}
   135	
   136	// A logical group of 1--32 adjacent threads cooperatively processes one CSR
   137	// row. Adjacent threads read adjacent values and column indices, while the
   138	// read-only vector uses the GPU's read-only cache. Choosing the group width at
   139	// run time avoids wasting a full warp on very short rows without sacrificing
   140	// coalescing or scalability for long rows.
   141	template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
   142	__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
   143	void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   144	                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   145	                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   146	                   const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,
   147	                   double* __restrict__ out) {
   148	    static_assert(GROUP_SIZE >= 1 && GROUP_SIZE <= 32 &&
   149	                  (GROUP_SIZE & (GROUP_SIZE - 1)) == 0);
   150	
   151	    constexpr unsigned int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
   152	    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (GROUP_SIZE - 1);
   153	    const index_t firstRow =
   154	        blockIdx.x * groupsPerBlock + threadIdx.x / GROUP_SIZE;
   155	    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * groupsPerBlock;
   156	
   157	    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
   158	        const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
   159	        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   160	        double sum = 0.0;
   161	
   162	        for (index_t item = rowBegin + lane; item < rowEnd;
   163	             item += GROUP_SIZE) {
   164	            const index_t column = cols[item];
   165	            sum = fma(val[item], __ldg(vec + column), sum);
   166	        }
   167	
   168	        const unsigned int active = __activemask();
   169	#pragma unroll
   170	        for (unsigned int offset = GROUP_SIZE / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
   171	            sum += __shfl_down_sync(active, sum, offset, GROUP_SIZE);
   172	        }
   173	
   174	        if (lane == 0) {
   175	            out[row] = sum;
   176	        }
   177	    }
   178	}
   179	
   180	template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
   181	int kernelGridSize(const index_t rows, const int multiprocessors) {
   182	    constexpr int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
   183	    const uint64_t blocksForRows =
   184	        (static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) + groupsPerBlock - 1) / groupsPerBlock;
   185	
   186	    int activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor = 0;
   187	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(
   188	        &activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor, spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE>,
   189	        CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0));
   190	    const uint64_t residentBlocks =
   191	        static_cast<uint64_t>(activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor) * multiprocessors;
   192	    const uint64_t grid = blocksForRows < residentBlocks
   193	                              ? blocksForRows
   194	                              : residentBlocks;
   195	    return static_cast<int>(grid == 0 ? 1 : grid);
   196	}
   197	
   198	template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
   199	void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   200	                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   201	                      const index_t rows, double* out, const int blocks) {
   202	    spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
   203	        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out);
   204	}
   205	
   206	struct SpmvLaunchConfig {
   207	    unsigned int groupSize;
   208	    int blocks;
   209	};
   210	
   211	SpmvLaunchConfig makeSpmvLaunchConfig(const index_t rows,
   212	                                      const index_t nonzeros,
   213	                                      const int multiprocessors) {
   214	    const uint64_t averageItemsPerRow =
   215	        (static_cast<uint64_t>(nonzeros) + rows - 1) / rows;
   216	
   217	    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 1) {
   218	        return {1, kernelGridSize<1>(rows, multiprocessors)};
   219	    }
   220	    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 2) {
   221	        return {2, kernelGridSize<2>(rows, multiprocessors)};
   222	    }
   223	    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 4) {
   224	        return {4, kernelGridSize<4>(rows, multiprocessors)};
   225	    }
   226	    // Slightly overlap the nominal width ranges: for moderately ragged rows,
   227	    // a smaller group wastes fewer lanes and outperforms rounding directly up
   228	    // to the next power of two.
   229	    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 13) {
   230	        return {8, kernelGridSize<8>(rows, multiprocessors)};
   231	    }
   232	    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 52) {
   233	        return {16, kernelGridSize<16>(rows, multiprocessors)};
   234	    }
   235	    return {32, kernelGridSize<32>(rows, multiprocessors)};
   236	}
   237	
   238	void launchSpmv(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
   239	                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
   240	                const index_t rows, double* out,
   241	                const SpmvLaunchConfig config) {
   242	    switch (config.groupSize) {
   243	        case 1:
   244	            launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
   245	                                config.blocks);
   246	            break;
   247	        case 2:
   248	            launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
   249	                                config.blocks);
   250	            break;
   251	        case 4:
   252	            launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
   253	                                config.blocks);
   254	            break;
   255	        case 8:
   256	            launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
   257	                                config.blocks);
   258	            break;
   259	        case 16:
   260	            launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
   261	                                 config.blocks);
   262	            break;
   263	        default:
   264	            launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
   265	                                 config.blocks);
   266	            break;
   267	    }
   268	}
   269	
   270	// ****************************************************************************
   271	// Function: verifyResults
   272	//
   273	// Purpose:
   274	//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
   275	//
   276	// Arguments:
   277	//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
   278	//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
   279	//   size: number of elements per vector
   280	//
   281	// ****************************************************************************
   282	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
   283	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   284	        const double ref = reference[i];
   285	        const double res = result[i];
   286	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
   287	            // For very small values, check absolute error
   288	            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   289	                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
   290	                return false;
   291	            }
   292	        } else {
   293	            // Check relative error
   294	            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
   295	            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   296	                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
   297	                       i, ref, res, relError);
   298	                return false;
   299	            }
   300	        }
   301	    }
   302	    return true;
   303	}
   304	
   305	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   306	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   307	    printf("Options:\n");
   308	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
   309	    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
   310	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   311	    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
   312	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   313	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   314	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   315	}
   316	
   317	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   318	    index_t numRows = 1024;
   319	    index_t sparsity = 10;
   320	    index_t iterations = 10;
   321	    double maxVal = 1.0;
   322	    bool validate = false;
   323	    bool printResults = false;
   324	
   325	    // Parse command line arguments
   326	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   327	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   328	            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   329	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   330	            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   331	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   332	            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   333	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   334	            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
   335	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   336	            validate = true;
   337	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   338	            printResults = true;
   339	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   340	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   341	            return 0;
   342	        } else {
   343	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   344	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   345	            return 1;
   346	        }
   347	    }
   348	
   349	    if (numRows == 0 || sparsity == 0 || iterations == 0) {
   350	        std::fprintf(stderr, "-n, -s, and -i must all be greater than zero\n");
   351	        return 1;
   352	    }
   353	
   354	    // Calculate the number of non-zero elements without overflowing the CSR
   355	    // index type. The original matrix generator stores offsets as uint32_t.
   356	    const uint64_t matrixItems = static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * numRows;
   357	    const uint64_t nItems64 = matrixItems / sparsity;
   358	    if (nItems64 > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
   359	        std::fprintf(stderr,
   360	                     "Matrix has too many non-zero elements for 32-bit CSR indices\n");
   361	        return 1;
   362	    }
   363	    const index_t nItems = static_cast<index_t>(nItems64);
   364	
   365	    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   366	    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
   367	    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
   368	    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
   369	           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) /
   370	                                      static_cast<double>(matrixItems)));
   371	    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
   372	    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
   373	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   374	
   375	    // Allocate and initialize data structures
   376	    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
   377	    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
   378	    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
   379	    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
   380	    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector
   381	
   382	    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   383	    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
   384	    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
   385	    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);
   386	
   387	    // For validation, compute reference solution
   388	    std::vector<double> h_reference;
   389	    if (validate) {
   390	        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
   391	        h_reference.resize(numRows);
   392	        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
   393	                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
   394	    }
   395	
   396	    int device = 0;
   397	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
   398	    cudaDeviceProp deviceProperties{};
   399	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&deviceProperties, device));
   400	    std::printf("CUDA device: %s\n", deviceProperties.name);
   401	
   402	    double* d_val = allocateDevice<double>(nItems);
   403	    index_t* d_cols = allocateDevice<index_t>(nItems);
   404	    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = allocateDevice<index_t>(numRows + 1);
   405	    double* d_vec = allocateDevice<double>(numRows);
   406	    double* d_out = allocateDevice<double>(numRows);
   407	
   408	    if (nItems != 0) {
   409	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_val, h_val.data(),
   410	                              static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(double),
   411	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   412	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols, h_cols.data(),
   413	                              static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(index_t),
   414	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   415	    }
   416	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters, h_rowDelimiters.data(),
   417	                          static_cast<size_t>(numRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t),
   418	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   419	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec, h_vec.data(),
   420	                          static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double),
   421	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   422	
   423	    const SpmvLaunchConfig launchConfig = makeSpmvLaunchConfig(
   424	        numRows, nItems, deviceProperties.multiProcessorCount);
   425	
   426	    // Perform one untimed launch so context setup and lazy module loading do
   427	    // not pollute the benchmark. All benchmark iterations execute on CUDA.
   428	    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   429	    launchSpmv(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out,
   430	               launchConfig);
   431	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   432	
   433	    cudaEvent_t start;
   434	    cudaEvent_t end;
   435	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
   436	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&end));
   437	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
   438	
   439	    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   440	        launchSpmv(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out,
   441	                   launchConfig);
   442	    }
   443	
   444	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   445	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(end));
   446	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(end));
   447	
   448	    float durationMs = 0.0F;
   449	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&durationMs, start, end));
   450	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_out.data(), d_out,
   451	                          static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(double),
   452	                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   453	
   454	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
   455	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(end));
   456	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));
   457	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
   458	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
   459	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
   460	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
   461	
   462	    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", static_cast<double>(durationMs));
   463	    
   464	    // Calculate performance metrics
   465	    const double gflops =
   466	        (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (static_cast<double>(durationMs) * 1e6);
   467	    const double avgTime = durationMs / static_cast<double>(iterations);
   468	    
   469	    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
   470	    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   471	    
   472	    // Print results for external validation
   473	    if (printResults) {
   474	        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
   475	    }
   476	
   477	    // Validation
   478	    if (validate) {
   479	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   480	        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);
   481	
   482	        if (valid) {
   483	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   484	            return 0;
   485	        } else {
   486	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   487	            return 1;
   488	        }
   489	    }
   490	
   491	    return 0;
   492	}
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	project(spmv_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
     3

     5	set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
     6	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 17)
     7	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
     8	set(CMAKE_CUDA_EXTENSIONS OFF)
     9	
    10	# Compile for the GPU present at configure time unless the caller explicitly
    11	# supplies a deployment architecture list.
    12	if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    13	    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
    14	endif()
    15	
    16	add_executable(spmv spmv.cpp)
    17	set_source_files_properties(spmv.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
    18	set_target_properties(spmv PROPERTIES CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF)
    19	
    20	# Enable optimizations
    21	target_compile_options(spmv PRIVATE
    22	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CXX>:-O3;-march=native;-Wall;-Wextra>
    23	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--use_fast_math;--extra-device-vectorization;-Xcompiler=-Wall,-Wextra>
    24	)
    25

    27	target_link_libraries(spmv m)

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* const operation,
+               const char* const file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d for %s: %s\n", file, line,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__)
 
+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    // cudaMalloc does not accept a zero-byte allocation. Empty matrices still
+    // need a valid (but never dereferenced) pointer for kernel arguments.
+    const size_t allocationCount = count == 0 ? 1 : count;

+// A logical group of 1--32 adjacent threads cooperatively processes one CSR
+// row. Adjacent threads read adjacent values and column indices, while the
+// read-only vector uses the GPU's read-only cache. Choosing the group width at
+// run time avoids wasting a full warp on very short rows without sacrificing
+// coalescing or scalability for long rows.
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(GROUP_SIZE >= 1 && GROUP_SIZE <= 32 &&
+                  (GROUP_SIZE & (GROUP_SIZE - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (GROUP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t firstRow =
+        blockIdx.x * groupsPerBlock + threadIdx.x / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * groupsPerBlock;
+
+    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+
+        for (index_t item = rowBegin + lane; item < rowEnd;
+             item += GROUP_SIZE) {
+            const index_t column = cols[item];
+            sum = fma(val[item], __ldg(vec + column), sum);
+        }
+
+        const unsigned int active = __activemask();
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int offset = GROUP_SIZE / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(active, sum, offset, GROUP_SIZE);
+        }
+
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+int kernelGridSize(const index_t rows, const int multiprocessors) {
+    constexpr int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const uint64_t blocksForRows =
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) + groupsPerBlock - 1) / groupsPerBlock;
+
+    int activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(
+        &activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor, spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE>,
+        CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0));
+    const uint64_t residentBlocks =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor) * multiprocessors;
+    const uint64_t grid = blocksForRows < residentBlocks
+                              ? blocksForRows
+                              : residentBlocks;
+    return static_cast<int>(grid == 0 ? 1 : grid);
+}
+
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t rows, double* out, const int blocks) {
+    spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out);
+}
+
+struct SpmvLaunchConfig {
+    unsigned int groupSize;
+    int blocks;
+};
+
+SpmvLaunchConfig makeSpmvLaunchConfig(const index_t rows,
+                                      const index_t nonzeros,
+                                      const int multiprocessors) {
+    const uint64_t averageItemsPerRow =
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(nonzeros) + rows - 1) / rows;
+
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 1) {
+        return {1, kernelGridSize<1>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 2) {
+        return {2, kernelGridSize<2>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 4) {
+        return {4, kernelGridSize<4>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    // Slightly overlap the nominal width ranges: for moderately ragged rows,
+    // a smaller group wastes fewer lanes and outperforms rounding directly up
+    // to the next power of two.
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 13) {
+        return {8, kernelGridSize<8>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 52) {
+        return {16, kernelGridSize<16>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    return {32, kernelGridSize<32>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t rows, double* out,
+                const SpmvLaunchConfig config) {
+    switch (config.groupSize) {
+        case 1:
+            launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 2:
+            launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 4:
+            launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 8:
+            launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 16:
+            launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                 config.blocks);
+            break;
+        default:
+            launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                 config.blocks);
+            break;
+    }
+}

+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+// coalescing or scalability for long rows.
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,

+

+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (GROUP_SIZE - 1);

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* const operation,
+               const char* const file, const int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d for %s: %s\n", file, line,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__)
 
+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    // cudaMalloc does not accept a zero-byte allocation. Empty matrices still
+    // need a valid (but never dereferenced) pointer for kernel arguments.
+    const size_t allocationCount = count == 0 ? 1 : count;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer),
+                          allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
+    return pointer;
+}

+// A logical group of 1--32 adjacent threads cooperatively processes one CSR
+// row. Adjacent threads read adjacent values and column indices, while the
+// read-only vector uses the GPU's read-only cache. Choosing the group width at
+// run time avoids wasting a full warp on very short rows without sacrificing
+// coalescing or scalability for long rows.
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(GROUP_SIZE >= 1 && GROUP_SIZE <= 32 &&
+                  (GROUP_SIZE & (GROUP_SIZE - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (GROUP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t firstRow =
+        blockIdx.x * groupsPerBlock + threadIdx.x / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * groupsPerBlock;
+
+    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+
+        for (index_t item = rowBegin + lane; item < rowEnd;
+             item += GROUP_SIZE) {
+            const index_t column = cols[item];
+            sum = fma(val[item], __ldg(vec + column), sum);
+        }
+
+        const unsigned int active = __activemask();
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int offset = GROUP_SIZE / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(active, sum, offset, GROUP_SIZE);
+        }
+
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+int kernelGridSize(const index_t rows, const int multiprocessors) {
+    constexpr int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const uint64_t blocksForRows =
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) + groupsPerBlock - 1) / groupsPerBlock;
+
+    int activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(
+        &activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor, spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE>,
+        CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0));
+    const uint64_t residentBlocks =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor) * multiprocessors;
+    const uint64_t grid = blocksForRows < residentBlocks
+                              ? blocksForRows
+                              : residentBlocks;
+    return static_cast<int>(grid == 0 ? 1 : grid);
+}
+
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t rows, double* out, const int blocks) {
+    spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out);
+}
+
+struct SpmvLaunchConfig {
+    unsigned int groupSize;
+    int blocks;
+};
+
+SpmvLaunchConfig makeSpmvLaunchConfig(const index_t rows,
+                                      const index_t nonzeros,
+                                      const int multiprocessors) {
+    const uint64_t averageItemsPerRow =
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(nonzeros) + rows - 1) / rows;
+
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 1) {
+        return {1, kernelGridSize<1>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 2) {
+        return {2, kernelGridSize<2>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 4) {
+        return {4, kernelGridSize<4>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    // Slightly overlap the nominal width ranges: for moderately ragged rows,
+    // a smaller group wastes fewer lanes and outperforms rounding directly up
+    // to the next power of two.
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 13) {
+        return {8, kernelGridSize<8>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 52) {
+        return {16, kernelGridSize<16>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    return {32, kernelGridSize<32>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t rows, double* out,
+                const SpmvLaunchConfig config) {
+    switch (config.groupSize) {
+        case 1:
+            launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 2:
+            launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 4:
+            launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 8:
+            launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 16:
+            launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                 config.blocks);
+            break;
+        default:
+            launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                 config.blocks);
+            break;
+    }
+}
+

codex
Implemented unconditional CUDA SpMV while preserving the `spmv` executable name.

Key changes:

- Adaptive 1–32 thread cooperative CSR row processing with warp-shuffle reductions.
- Occupancy-aware launch sizing, coalesced accesses, read-only vector caching, and FMA.
- GPU allocation/transfers outside timing; CUDA events provide accurate kernel timing.
- CPU implementation retained only for optional validation.
- CMake now enables CUDA, native GPU architecture, and optimized compilation.

Files changed:

- [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_cuda_r3/spmv/spmv.cpp:136)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_cuda_r3/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Fresh configure/build succeeded. All six adaptive kernel paths, empty matrices, result printing, and the default workload passed CPU-reference validation. On the RTX 3090, a 4096² workload reached approximately 86 GFLOPS.

+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__)
 
+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    // cudaMalloc does not accept a zero-byte allocation. Empty matrices still
+    // need a valid (but never dereferenced) pointer for kernel arguments.
+    const size_t allocationCount = count == 0 ? 1 : count;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer),
+                          allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
+    return pointer;
+}

+// A logical group of 1--32 adjacent threads cooperatively processes one CSR
+// row. Adjacent threads read adjacent values and column indices, while the
+// read-only vector uses the GPU's read-only cache. Choosing the group width at
+// run time avoids wasting a full warp on very short rows without sacrificing
+// coalescing or scalability for long rows.
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                   const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,
+                   double* __restrict__ out) {
+    static_assert(GROUP_SIZE >= 1 && GROUP_SIZE <= 32 &&
+                  (GROUP_SIZE & (GROUP_SIZE - 1)) == 0);
+
+    constexpr unsigned int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (GROUP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t firstRow =
+        blockIdx.x * groupsPerBlock + threadIdx.x / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const index_t rowStride = gridDim.x * groupsPerBlock;
+
+    for (index_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+
+        for (index_t item = rowBegin + lane; item < rowEnd;
+             item += GROUP_SIZE) {
+            const index_t column = cols[item];
+            sum = fma(val[item], __ldg(vec + column), sum);
+        }
+
+        const unsigned int active = __activemask();
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int offset = GROUP_SIZE / 2; offset != 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(active, sum, offset, GROUP_SIZE);
+        }
+
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+int kernelGridSize(const index_t rows, const int multiprocessors) {
+    constexpr int groupsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / GROUP_SIZE;
+    const uint64_t blocksForRows =
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) + groupsPerBlock - 1) / groupsPerBlock;
+
+    int activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(
+        &activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor, spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE>,
+        CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0));
+    const uint64_t residentBlocks =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(activeBlocksPerMultiprocessor) * multiprocessors;
+    const uint64_t grid = blocksForRows < residentBlocks
+                              ? blocksForRows
+                              : residentBlocks;
+    return static_cast<int>(grid == 0 ? 1 : grid);
+}
+
+template <unsigned int GROUP_SIZE>
+void launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                      const index_t rows, double* out, const int blocks) {
+    spmvCsrKernel<GROUP_SIZE><<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out);
+}
+
+struct SpmvLaunchConfig {
+    unsigned int groupSize;
+    int blocks;
+};
+
+SpmvLaunchConfig makeSpmvLaunchConfig(const index_t rows,
+                                      const index_t nonzeros,
+                                      const int multiprocessors) {
+    const uint64_t averageItemsPerRow =
+        (static_cast<uint64_t>(nonzeros) + rows - 1) / rows;
+
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 1) {
+        return {1, kernelGridSize<1>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 2) {
+        return {2, kernelGridSize<2>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 4) {
+        return {4, kernelGridSize<4>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    // Slightly overlap the nominal width ranges: for moderately ragged rows,
+    // a smaller group wastes fewer lanes and outperforms rounding directly up
+    // to the next power of two.
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 13) {
+        return {8, kernelGridSize<8>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    if (averageItemsPerRow <= 52) {
+        return {16, kernelGridSize<16>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+    }
+    return {32, kernelGridSize<32>(rows, multiprocessors)};
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const double* val, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec,
+                const index_t rows, double* out,
+                const SpmvLaunchConfig config) {
+    switch (config.groupSize) {
+        case 1:
+            launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 2:
+            launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 4:
+            launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 8:
+            launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                config.blocks);
+            break;
+        case 16:
+            launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                 config.blocks);
+            break;
+        default:
+            launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, rows, out,
+                                 config.blocks);
+            break;
+    }
+}
+

 //

Implemented unconditional CUDA SpMV while preserving the `spmv` executable name.

Key changes:

- Adaptive 1–32 thread cooperative CSR row processing with warp-shuffle reductions.
- Occupancy-aware launch sizing, coalesced accesses, read-only vector caching, and FMA.
- GPU allocation/transfers outside timing; CUDA events provide accurate kernel timing.
- CPU implementation retained only for optional validation.
- CMake now enables CUDA, native GPU architecture, and optimized compilation.
